│ struct FB_BitField { uint32_t offset;                   /* beginning of      │
│ bitfield        / uint32_t length;                      / length of bitfield │
│ */ };                                                                        │
│                                                                              │
│ struct FB_ScreenInfo { uint32_t xres;                   /* visible           │
│ resolution           / uint32_t yres; uint32_t xres_virtual;         /       │
│ virtual resolution            */ uint32_t yres_virtual;                      │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│  uint32_t bits_per_pixel;        /* guess what           */                  │
│                                                                              │
│                          /* &gt;1 = FOURCC           */                      │
│  struct FB_BitField red;     /* bitfield in s_Fb mem if true color, */       │
│  struct FB_BitField green;   /* else only length is significant */           │
│  struct FB_BitField blue;                                                    │
│  struct FB_BitField transp;  /* transparency         */                      │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│ };                                                                           │
│                                                                              │
│ static struct FB_ScreenInfo s_Fb; int fb_scaling = 1; int usemouse = 0;      │
│                                                                              │
│ #ifdef CMAP256                                                               │
│                                                                              │
│ boolean palette_changed; struct color colors[256];                           │
│                                                                              │
│ #else  // CMAP256                                                            │
│                                                                              │
│ static struct color colors[256];                                             │
│                                                                              │
│ #endif  // CMAP256                                                           │

│ void I_GetEvent(void);                                                       │

│ // Mouse acceleration // // This emulates some of the behavior of DOS mouse  │
│ drivers by increasing // the speed when the mouse is moved fast. // // The   │
│ mouse input values are input directly to the game, but when // the values    │
│ exceed the value of mouse_threshold, they are multiplied // by               │
│ mouse_acceleration to increase the speed.                                    │
│                                                                              │
│ float mouse_acceleration = 2.0; int mouse_threshold = 10;                    │
│                                                                              │
│ // Gamma correction level to use                                             │
│                                                                              │
│ int usegamma = 0;                                                            │
│                                                                              │
│ typedef struct { byte r; byte g; byte b; } col_t;                            │
│                                                                              │
│ // Palette converted to RGB565                                               │
│                                                                              │
│ static uint16_t rgb565_palette[256];                                         │
│                                                                              │
│ void cmap_to_rgb565(uint16_t * out, uint8_t * in, int in_pixels) { int i, j; │
│ struct color c; uint16_t r, g, b;                                            │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│  for (i = 0; i &lt; in_pixels; i++)                                          │
│  {                                                                           │
│      c = colors[*in];                                                        │
│      r = ((uint16_t)(c.r &gt;&gt; 3)) &lt;&lt; 11;                           │
│      g = ((uint16_t)(c.g &gt;&gt; 2)) &lt;&lt; 5;                            │
│      b = ((uint16_t)(c.b &gt;&gt; 3)) &lt;&lt; 0;                            │
│      *out = (r | g | b);                                                     │
│                                                                              │
│      in++;                                                                   │
│      for (j = 0; j &lt; fb_scaling; j++) {                                   │
│          out++;                                                              │
│      }                                                                       │
│  }                                                                           │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│ }                                                                            │
│                                                                              │
│ void cmap_to_fb(uint8_t * out, uint8_t * in, int in_pixels) { int i, j, k;   │
│ struct color c; uint32_t pix; uint16_t r, g, b;                              │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│  for (i = 0; i &lt; in_pixels; i++)                                          │
│  {                                                                           │
│      c = colors[*in];  /* R:8 G:8 B:8 format! */                             │
│      r = (uint16_t)(c.r &gt;&gt; (8 - s_Fb.red.length));                     │
│      g = (uint16_t)(c.g &gt;&gt; (8 - s_Fb.green.length));                   │
│      b = (uint16_t)(c.b &gt;&gt; (8 - s_Fb.blue.length));                    │
│      pix = r &lt;&lt; s_Fb.red.offset;                                       │
│      pix |= g &lt;&lt; s_Fb.green.offset;                                    │
│      pix |= b &lt;&lt; s_Fb.blue.offset;                                     │
│                                                                              │
│      for (k = 0; k &lt; fb_scaling; k++) {                                   │
│          for (j = 0; j &lt; s_Fb.bits_per_pixel/8; j++) {                    │
│              *out = (pix &gt;&gt; (j*8));                                    │
│              out++;                                                          │
│          }                                                                   │
│      }                                                                       │
│      in++;                                                                   │
│  }                                                                           │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│ }                                                                            │
│                                                                              │
│ void I_InitGraphics (void) { int i;                                          │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│  memset(&amp;s_Fb, 0, sizeof(struct FB_ScreenInfo));                         │
│  s_Fb.xres = DOOMGENERIC_RESX;                                               │
│  s_Fb.yres = DOOMGENERIC_RESY;                                               │
│  s_Fb.xres_virtual = s_Fb.xres;                                              │
│  s_Fb.yres_virtual = s_Fb.yres;                                              │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│ #ifdef CMAP256                                                               │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│  s_Fb.bits_per_pixel = 8;                                                    │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│ #else  // CMAP256                                                            │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│  s_Fb.bits_per_pixel = 32;                                                   │
│                                                                              │
│  s_Fb.blue.length = 8;                                                       │
│  s_Fb.green.length = 8;                                                      │
│  s_Fb.red.length = 8;                                                        │
│  s_Fb.transp.length = 8;                                                     │
│                                                                              │
│  s_Fb.blue.offset = 0;                                                       │
│  s_Fb.green.offset = 8;                                                      │
│  s_Fb.red.offset = 16;                                                       │
│  s_Fb.transp.offset = 24;                                                    │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│ #endif  // CMAP256                                                           │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│  printf(&quot;I_InitGraphics: framebuffer: x_res: %d, y_res: %d, x_virtual:  │
│  %d, y_virtual: %d, bpp: %d\n&quot;,                                         │
│          s_Fb.xres, s_Fb.yres, s_Fb.xres_virtual, s_Fb.yres_virtual,         │
│  s_Fb.bits_per_pixel);                                                       │
│                                                                              │
│  printf(&quot;I_InitGraphics: framebuffer: RGBA: %d%d%d%d, red_off: %d,      │
│  green_off: %d, blue_off: %d, transp_off: %d\n&quot;,                        │
│          s_Fb.red.length, s_Fb.green.length, s_Fb.blue.length,               │
│  s_Fb.transp.length, s_Fb.red.offset, s_Fb.green.offset, s_Fb.blue.offset,   │
│  s_Fb.transp.offset);                                                        │
│                                                                              │
│  printf(&quot;I_InitGraphics: DOOM screen size: w x h: %d x %d\n&quot;,      │
│  SCREENWIDTH, SCREENHEIGHT);                                                 │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│  i = M_CheckParmWithArgs(&quot;-scaling&quot;, 1);                           │
│  if (i &gt; 0) {                                                             │
│      i = atoi(myargv[i + 1]);                                                │
│      fb_scaling = i;                                                         │
│      printf(&quot;I_InitGraphics: Scaling factor: %d\n&quot;, fb_scaling);   │
│  } else {                                                                    │
│      fb_scaling = s_Fb.xres / SCREENWIDTH;                                   │
│      if (s_Fb.yres / SCREENHEIGHT &lt; fb_scaling)                           │
│          fb_scaling = s_Fb.yres / SCREENHEIGHT;                              │
│      printf(&quot;I_InitGraphics: Auto-scaling factor: %d\n&quot;,           │
│  fb_scaling);                                                                │
│  }                                                                           │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│  /* Allocate screen to draw to */                                            │
│  I_VideoBuffer = (byte*)Z_Malloc (SCREENWIDTH * SCREENHEIGHT, PU_STATIC,     │
│  NULL);  // For DOOM to draw on                                              │
│                                                                              │
│  screenvisible = true;                                                       │
│                                                                              │

│ <output> /**                                                                 │
│                                                                              │
│  • MIPS Virtual Machine in JavaScript                                        │
│  • This VM implements the core MIPS instruction set for use in a             │
│    MIPS-to-JavaScript compiler.                                              │
│  • It handles register operations, memory access, and program flow control.  │
│    */                                                                        │
│                                                                              │
│ const fs = require('fs');                                                    │
│                                                                              │
│ class MIPSVM { constructor() { // Initialize 32 general-purpose registers    │
│ (R0-R31) // R0 is always 0 in MIPS this.registers = new Array(32).fill(0);   │
│ this.registers[29] = 0x7FFFFFFC                                              │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│  // Register names for debugging                                             │
│  this.regNames = [                                                           │
│    &quot;zero&quot;, &quot;at&quot;, &quot;v0&quot;, &quot;v1&quot;,         │
│  &quot;a0&quot;, &quot;a1&quot;, &quot;a2&quot;, &quot;a3&quot;,             │
│    &quot;t0&quot;, &quot;t1&quot;, &quot;t2&quot;, &quot;t3&quot;,           │
│  &quot;t4&quot;, &quot;t5&quot;, &quot;t6&quot;, &quot;t7&quot;,             │
│    &quot;s0&quot;, &quot;s1&quot;, &quot;s2&quot;, &quot;s3&quot;,           │
│  &quot;s4&quot;, &quot;s5&quot;, &quot;s6&quot;, &quot;s7&quot;,             │
│    &quot;t8&quot;, &quot;t9&quot;, &quot;k0&quot;, &quot;k1&quot;,           │
│  &quot;gp&quot;, &quot;sp&quot;, &quot;fp&quot;, &quot;ra&quot;              │
│  ];                                                                          │
│                                                                              │
│  // Special registers                                                        │
│  this.hi = 0;  // High result register                                       │
│  this.lo = 0;  // Low result register                                        │
│                                                                              │
│  // Program counter                                                          │
│  this.pc = 0;                                                                │
│                                                                              │
│  // Memory (simplified as an object mapping addresses to values)             │
│  this.memory = {};                                                           │
│                                                                              │
│  // Exception program counter                                                │
│  this.epc = 0;                                                               │
│                                                                              │
│  // File descriptors for syscalls                                            │
│  this.fileDescriptors = {                                                    │
│    0: process.stdin.fd,   // Standard input                                  │
│    1: process.stdout.fd,  // Standard output                                 │
│    2: process.stderr.fd   // Standard error                                  │
│  };                                                                          │
│  this.fdToStreamMap = {                                                      │
│    [process.stdin.fd]: process.stdin,                                        │
│    [process.stdout.fd]: process.stdout,                                      │
│    [process.stderr.fd]: process.stderr                                       │
│  };                                                                          │
│  this.fileCache = {};                                                        │
│  this.fdCounter = 3;  // Start after stdin, stdout, stderr                   │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│ }                                                                            │
│                                                                              │
│ // Helper methods                                                            │
│                                                                              │
│ signExtend16(value) { // Sign-extend a 16-bit value to 32 bits value &=      │
│ 0xFFFF; // Ensure it's 16 bits return (value & 0x8000) ? (value |            │
│ 0xFFFF0000) : value; }                                                       │
│                                                                              │
│ // Print the current state of the registers printRegisters() { for (let i =  │
│ 0; i < 32; i++) { console.log($${i} (${this.regNames[i]}) =                  │
│ 0x${this.registers[i].toString(16).padStart(8, &#x27;0&#x27;)}               │
│ (${this.registers[i]})); } console.log(HI =                                  │
│ 0x${this.hi.toString(16).padStart(8, &#x27;0&#x27;)} (${this.hi}));          │
│ console.log(LO = 0x${this.lo.toString(16).padStart(8, &#x27;0&#x27;)}        │
│ (${this.lo})); console.log(PC = 0x${this.pc.toString(16).padStart(8,         │
│ &#x27;0&#x27;)}); }                                                          │
│                                                                              │
│ // ---- ALU Operations ----                                                  │
│                                                                              │
│ add(rs, rt, rd) { // ADD rd,rs,rt - Add (with overflow) const result =       │
│ (this.registers[rs] + this.registers[rt]) | 0; // Force 32-bit signed math   │
│ this.registers[rd] = result; // Note: In a real MIPS CPU, this would check   │
│ for overflow }                                                               │
│                                                                              │
│ addi(rt, rs, imm) { // ADDI rt,rs,imm - Add Immediate (with overflow) const  │
│ signExtImm = this.signExtend16(imm); const result = (this.registers[rs] +    │
│ signExtImm) | 0; // Force 32-bit signed math this.registers[rt] = result; // │
│ Note: In a real MIPS CPU, this would check for overflow }                    │
│                                                                              │
│ addiu(rt, rs, imm) { // ADDIU rt,rs,imm - Add Immediate Unsigned (no         │
│ overflow) const signExtImm = this.signExtend16(imm); this.registers[rt] =    │
│ (this.registers[rs] + signExtImm) >>> 0; // Force 32-bit unsigned math }     │
│                                                                              │
│ addu(rs, rt, rd) { // ADDU rd,rs,rt - Add Unsigned (no overflow)             │
│ this.registers[rd] = (this.registers[rs] + this.registers[rt]) >>> 0; //     │
│ Force 32-bit unsigned math }                                                 │
│                                                                              │
│ and(rs, rt, rd) { // AND rd,rs,rt - Bitwise AND this.registers[rd] =         │
│ (this.registers[rs] & this.registers[rt]) >>> 0; }                           │
│                                                                              │
│ andi(rt, rs, imm) { // ANDI rt,rs,imm - Bitwise AND Immediate // Note:       │
│ Zero-extended for this operation this.registers[rt] = this.registers[rs] &   │
│ (imm & 0xFFFF); }                                                            │
│                                                                              │
│ lui(rt, imm) { // LUI rt,imm - Load Upper Immediate this.registers[rt] =     │
│ (imm & 0xFFFF) << 16; }                                                      │
│                                                                              │
│ nor(rs, rt, rd) { // NOR rd,rs,rt - Bitwise NOR this.registers[rd] =         │
│ ~(this.registers[rs] | this.registers[rt]) >>> 0; // Force 32-bit unsigned } │
│                                                                              │
│ or(rs, rt, rd) { // OR rd,rs,rt - Bitwise OR this.registers[rd] =            │
│ (this.registers[rs] | this.registers[rt]) >>> 0; }                           │
│                                                                              │
│ ori(rt, rs, imm) { // ORI rt,rs,imm - Bitwise OR Immediate // Note:          │
│ Zero-extended for this operation this.registers[rt] = this.registers[rs] |   │
│ (imm & 0xFFFF); }                                                            │
│                                                                              │
│ slt(rs, rt, rd) { // SLT rd,rs,rt - Set on Less Than (signed) // Convert to  │
│ signed values for comparison let valRs = this.registers[rs]; let valRt =     │
│ this.registers[rt];                                                          │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│  // JavaScript handles signed comparison natively                            │
│  this.registers[rd] = (valRs | 0) &lt; (valRt | 0) ? 1 : 0;                  │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│ }                                                                            │
│                                                                              │
│ slti(rt, rs, imm) { // SLTI rt,rs,imm - Set on Less Than Immediate (signed)  │
│ const signExtImm = this.signExtend16(imm); // JavaScript handles signed      │
│ comparison natively this.registers[rt] = (this.registers[rs] | 0) <          │
│ (signExtImm | 0) ? 1 : 0; }                                                  │
│                                                                              │
│ sltiu(rt, rs, imm) { // SLTIU rt,rs,imm - Set on Less Than Immediate         │
│ Unsigned const signExtImm = this.signExtend16(imm) >>> 0; // Force unsigned  │
│ this.registers[rt] = (this.registers[rs] >>> 0) < signExtImm ? 1 : 0; }      │
│                                                                              │
│ sltu(rs, rt, rd) { // SLTU rd,rs,rt - Set on Less Than Unsigned              │
│ this.registers[rd] = (this.registers[rs] >>> 0) < (this.registers[rt] >>> 0) │
│ ? 1 : 0; }                                                                   │
│                                                                              │
│ sub(rs, rt, rd) { // SUB rd,rs,rt - Subtract (with overflow)                 │
│ this.registers[rd] = (this.registers[rs] - this.registers[rt]) | 0; // Force │
│ 32-bit signed // Note: In a real MIPS CPU, this would check for overflow }   │
│                                                                              │
│ subu(rs, rt, rd) { // SUBU rd,rs,rt - Subtract Unsigned (no overflow)        │
│ this.registers[rd] = (this.registers[rs] - this.registers[rt]) >>> 0; //     │
│ Force 32-bit unsigned }                                                      │
│                                                                              │
│ xor(rs, rt, rd) { // XOR rd,rs,rt - Bitwise XOR this.registers[rd] =         │
│ this.registers[rs] ^ this.registers[rt]; }                                   │
│                                                                              │
│ xori(rt, rs, imm) { // XORI rt,rs,imm - Bitwise XOR Immediate // Note:       │
│ Zero-extended for this operation this.registers[rt] = this.registers[rs] ^   │
│ (imm & 0xFFFF); }                                                            │
│                                                                              │
│ // ---- Shifter Operations ----                                              │
│                                                                              │
│ sll(rt, rd, sa) { // SLL rd,rt,sa - Shift Left Logical this.registers[rd] =  │
│ (this.registers[rt] << sa) >>> 0; // Force 32-bit unsigned }                 │
│                                                                              │
│ sllv(rs, rt, rd) { // SLLV rd,rt,rs - Shift Left Logical Variable const      │
│ shift = this.registers[rs] & 0x1F; // Only bottom 5 bits used                │
│ this.registers[rd] = (this.registers[rt] << shift) >>> 0; // Force 32-bit    │
│ unsigned }                                                                   │
│                                                                              │
│ sra(rt, rd, sa) { // SRA rd,rt,sa - Shift Right Arithmetic // JavaScript >>  │
│ is arithmetic shift right this.registers[rd] = (this.registers[rt] >> sa) |  │
│ 0; // Force 32-bit signed }                                                  │
│                                                                              │
│ srav(rs, rt, rd) { // SRAV rd,rt,rs - Shift Right Arithmetic Variable const  │
│ shift = this.registers[rs] & 0x1F; // Only bottom 5 bits used                │
│ this.registers[rd] = (this.registers[rt] >> shift) | 0; // Force 32-bit      │
│ signed }                                                                     │
│                                                                              │
│ srl(rt, rd, sa) { // SRL rd,rt,sa - Shift Right Logical // JavaScript >>> is │
│ logical shift right this.registers[rd] = (this.registers[rt] >>> sa) >>> 0;  │
│ // Force 32-bit unsigned }                                                   │
│                                                                              │
│ srlv(rs, rt, rd) { // SRLV rd,rt,rs - Shift Right Logical Variable const     │
│ shift = this.registers[rs] & 0x1F; // Only bottom 5 bits used                │
│ this.registers[rd] = (this.registers[rt] >>> shift) >>> 0; // Force 32-bit   │
│ unsigned }                                                                   │
│                                                                              │
│ // ---- Multiply Operations ----                                             │
│                                                                              │
│ div(rs, rt) { // DIV rs,rt - Divide (signed) if (this.registers[rt] === 0) { │
│ // Division by zero - implementation defined this.lo = 0; this.hi = 0;       │
│ return; }                                                                    │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│  // JavaScript handles signed division natively                              │
│  this.lo = ((this.registers[rs] | 0) / (this.registers[rt] | 0)) | 0; //     │
│  Quotient                                                                    │
│  this.hi = ((this.registers[rs] | 0) % (this.registers[rt] | 0)) | 0; //     │
│  Remainder                                                                   │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│ }                                                                            │
│                                                                              │
│ divu(rs, rt) { // DIVU rs,rt - Divide Unsigned if (this.registers[rt] === 0) │
│ { // Division by zero - implementation defined this.lo = 0; this.hi = 0;     │
│ return; }                                                                    │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│  const unsignedRs = this.registers[rs] &gt;&gt;&gt; 0;                       │
│  const unsignedRt = this.registers[rt] &gt;&gt;&gt; 0;                       │
│                                                                              │
│  this.lo = Math.floor(unsignedRs / unsignedRt) &gt;&gt;&gt; 0; // Quotient   │
│  this.hi = (unsignedRs % unsignedRt) &gt;&gt;&gt; 0; // Remainder            │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│ }                                                                            │
│                                                                              │
│ mfhi(rd) { // MFHI rd - Move From HI this.registers[rd] = this.hi; }         │
│                                                                              │
│ mflo(rd) { // MFLO rd - Move From LO this.registers[rd] = this.lo; }         │
│                                                                              │
│ </output>                                                                    │

│ // ---- System Call Handler ----                                             │
│                                                                              │
│ handleSyscall() { // Store the address of the current instruction in case we │
│ need it this.epc = this.pc - 4;                                              │
│                                                                              │
│ // Get syscall number from $v0 (register 2) const syscallNum =               │
│ this.registers[2];                                                           │
│                                                                              │
│ // Ensure Node.js fs and process modules are available // This assumes the   │
│ code is running in a Node.js environment const fs = require('fs'); const     │
│ process = require('process');                                                │
│                                                                              │
│ // Initialize file descriptors tracking if not already done if (syscallNum   │
│ === 0) {  // SYS_read // Get arguments from registers const fd =             │
│ this.fileDescriptors[this.registers[4]];            // File descriptor const │
│ bufferAddr = this.registers[5];    // Buffer address in MIPS memory const    │
│ count = this.registers[6];         // Number of bytes to read                │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│  try {                                                                       │
│    // Check if we&#x27;re reading from stdin                                 │
│    if (fd === 0) {                                                           │
│      // Reading from stdin is blocking in a real implementation              │
│      // This is a simplification - in a real VM you&#x27;d need to handle    │
│  this asynchronously                                                         │
│      console.log(&quot;Reading from stdin not fully implemented in this      │
│  example&quot;);                                                             │
│      const testInput = &quot;Test input\n&quot;;                             │
│      const bytesRead = Math.min(testInput.length, count);                    │
│                                                                              │
│      for (let i = 0; i &lt; bytesRead; i++) {                                │
│        this.writeMemoryByte(bufferAddr + i, testInput.charCodeAt(i));        │
│      }                                                                       │
│                                                                              │
│      this.registers[2] = bytesRead;                                          │
│    } else {                                                                  │
│        // For regular files, read from our cached file data                  │
│    //console.log(this.fileCache, fd)                                         │
│      if (!this.fileCache[fd]) {                                              │
│        this.registers[2] = -1;  // Error: file not in cache                  │
│        return;                                                               │
│      }                                                                       │
│                                                                              │
│      const fileInfo = this.fileCache[fd];                                    │
│      const fileData = fileInfo.data;                                         │
│      const position = fileInfo.position;                                     │
│                                                                              │
│      // Calculate how many bytes we can actually read                        │
│      const bytesAvailable = fileData.length - position;                      │
│      const bytesRead = Math.min(count, bytesAvailable);                      │
│                                                                              │
│      // Copy data from file cache to MIPS memory                             │
│      for (let i = 0; i &lt; bytesRead; i++) {                                │
│        this.writeMemoryByte(bufferAddr + i, fileData[position + i]);         │
│      }                                                                       │
│                                                                              │
│      // Update file position                                                 │
│      fileInfo.position += bytesRead;                                         │
│                                                                              │
│      // Return number of bytes read                                          │
│      this.registers[2] = bytesRead;                                          │
│    }                                                                         │
│  } catch (error) {                                                           │
│    console.error(`SYS_read error: ${error.message}`);                        │
│    this.registers[2] = -1;  // Error code                                    │
│  }                                                                           │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│ } else if (syscallNum === 1) {  // SYS_write // Get arguments from registers │
│ const fd = this.fileDescriptors[this.registers[4]];           // File        │
│ descriptor const bufferAddr = this.registers[5];   // Buffer address in MIPS │
│ memory const count = this.registers[6];        // Number of bytes to write   │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│  try {                                                                       │
│    // Create a buffer to hold the data to write                              │
│    const buffer = Buffer.alloc(count);                                       │
│                                                                              │
│    // Read data from MIPS VM memory into the buffer                          │
│    for (let i = 0; i &lt; count; i++) {                                      │
│      buffer[i] = this.readMemoryByte(bufferAddr + i);                        │
│    }                                                                         │
│                                                                              │
│    // For files opened for writing, we&#x27;ll buffer the data and write on  │
│  close                                                                       │
│    if (this.fileCache[fd] &amp;&amp;                                         │
│  this.fileCache[fd].mode.includes(&#x27;w&#x27;)) {                          │
│      const fileInfo = this.fileCache[fd];                                    │
│                                                                              │
│      // If the file was opened in append mode, move position to end          │
│      if (fileInfo.mode.includes(&#x27;a&#x27;)) {                            │
│        fileInfo.position = fileInfo.data.length;                             │
│      }                                                                       │
│                                                                              │
│      // Ensure the data buffer is large enough                               │
│      const newSize = Math.max(fileInfo.position + count,                     │
│  fileInfo.data.length);                                                      │
│      if (newSize &gt; fileInfo.data.length) {                                │
│        const newData = Buffer.alloc(newSize);                                │
│        fileInfo.data.copy(newData, 0, 0, fileInfo.data.length);              │
│        fileInfo.data = newData;                                              │
│      }                                                                       │
│                                                                              │
│      // Copy the new data into the buffer at the current position            │
│      buffer.copy(fileInfo.data, fileInfo.position, 0, count);                │
│      fileInfo.position += count;                                             │
│      fileInfo.modified = true;                                               │
│                                                                              │
│      this.registers[2] = count;  // Return bytes written                     │
│    } else {                                                                  │
│      // For stdout, stderr, or other uncached files, use direct write        │
│      const bytesWritten = fs.writeSync(fd, buffer, 0, count, null);          │
│      this.registers[2] = bytesWritten;                                       │
│    }                                                                         │
│  } catch (error) {                                                           │
│    console.error(`SYS_write error: ${error.message}`);                       │
│    this.registers[2] = -1;  // Error code                                    │
│  }                                                                           │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│ } else if (syscallNum === 2) {  // SYS_open console.log("Open!"); // Get     │
│ arguments from registers const pathnameAddr = this.registers[4];   //        │
│ Pathname address in MIPS memory const flags = this.registers[5];          // │
│ Open flags const mode = this.registers[6];           // File permissions     │
│ mode                                                                         │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│  // Read the null-terminated pathname string from MIPS memory                │
│  let pathname = &quot;&quot;;                                                │
│  let i = 0;                                                                  │
│  let byte;                                                                   │
│  while ((byte = this.readMemoryByte(pathnameAddr + i)) !== 0) {              │
│    pathname += String.fromCharCode(byte);                                    │
│    i++;                                                                      │
│    if (i &gt; 10240) break;  // Safety limit                                 │
│  }                                                                           │
│                                                                              │
│  try {                                                                       │
│    // Define constants for the flags (Linux/MIPS values)                     │
│    const O_RDONLY = 0;                                                       │
│    const O_WRONLY = 1;                                                       │
│    const O_RDWR = 2;                                                         │
│    const O_CREAT = 64;                                                       │
│    const O_EXCL = 128;                                                       │
│    const O_TRUNC = 512;                                                      │
│    const O_APPEND = 1024;                                                    │
│                                                                              │
│    // Convert MIPS/Linux flags to Node.js fs flags                           │
│    let fsFlags = &quot;&quot;;                                               │
│                                                                              │
│    // Handle basic access modes                                              │
│    const accessMode = flags &amp; 0x3;  // Extract access mode bits          │
│    if (accessMode === O_RDONLY) {                                            │
│      fsFlags = &quot;r&quot;;                                                │
│    } else if (accessMode === O_WRONLY) {                                     │
│      fsFlags = &quot;w&quot;;                                                │
│    } else if (accessMode === O_RDWR) {                                       │
│      fsFlags = &quot;r+&quot;;                                               │
│    }                                                                         │
│                                                                              │
│    // Handle creation, truncation, and append flags                          │
│    if (flags &amp; O_CREAT) {                                                │
│      if (fsFlags === &quot;r&quot;) fsFlags = &quot;wx&quot;;                │
│      else if (fsFlags === &quot;r+&quot;) fsFlags = &quot;wx+&quot;;         │
│    }                                                                         │
│                                                                              │
│    if (flags &amp; O_TRUNC) {                                                │
│      if (fsFlags === &quot;r+&quot;) fsFlags = &quot;w+&quot;;  // r+        │
│  doesn&#x27;t truncate, w+ does                                              │
│    }                                                                         │
│                                                                              │
│    if (flags &amp; O_APPEND) {                                               │
│      if (fsFlags === &quot;w&quot;) fsFlags = &quot;a&quot;;                 │
│      else if (fsFlags === &quot;w+&quot;) fsFlags = &quot;a+&quot;;          │
│    }                                                                         │
│                                                                              │
│    // Open the file                                                          │
│    const fd = fs.openSync(pathname, fsFlags, mode);                          │
│                                                                              │
│    this.fileDescriptors[this.fdCounter++]  = fd                              │
│                                                                              │

│ static unsigned char convertToDoomKey(unsigned int key) { switch (key) {     │
│ case '\r':  // Return key key = KEY_ENTER; break; case 27:    // Escape key  │
│ key = KEY_ESCAPE; break; case 0x4B:  // Left arrow key = KEY_LEFTARROW;      │
│ break; case 0x4D:  // Right arrow key = KEY_RIGHTARROW; break; case 0x48:    │
│ // Up arrow key = KEY_UPARROW; break; case 0x50:  // Down arrow key =        │
│ KEY_DOWNARROW; break; case 0x1D:  // Ctrl key = KEY_FIRE; break; case ' ':   │

│ Right Shift key = KEY_RSHIFT; break; default: key = (key); break; }          │

│ static void addKeyToQueue(int pressed, unsigned int keyCode) { unsigned char │
│ key = convertToDoomKey(keyCode);                                             │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│  unsigned short keyData = (pressed &lt;&lt; 8) | key;                        │
│                                                                              │
│  s_KeyQueue[s_KeyQueueWriteIndex] = keyData;                                 │
│  s_KeyQueueWriteIndex++;                                                     │
│  s_KeyQueueWriteIndex %= KEYQUEUE_SIZE;                                      │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│ }                                                                            │
│                                                                              │
│ // Function to write BMP file static void writeBMPFile(const char* filename, │
│ uint32_t* buffer, int width, int height) { uint32_t image_size = width *     │
│ height * 4;  // 4 bytes per pixel (BGRA) uint32_t file_size = 54 +           │
│ image_size;      // Headers + image data                                     │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│  // Allocate and initialize the buffer to 0                                  │
│  uint8_t* bmp = (uint8_t*)malloc(file_size+2);                               │
│  for (int i = 0; i &lt; 1024; i++) {                                         │
│    bmp[i] = 0;                                                               │
│  }                                                                           │
│  if (!bmp) {                                                                 │
│      fprintf(stderr, &quot;Memory allocation failed\n&quot;);                │
│      return;                                                                 │
│  }                                                                           │
│                                                                              │
│  bmp = bmp+2;                                                                │
│                                                                              │
│  // Use uint32_t array for headers (with proper byte offsets)                │
│  uint16_t* signature = (uint16_t*)bmp;                                       │
│  uint32_t* header = (uint32_t*)(bmp + 2);                                    │
│                                                                              │
│  // File header (14 bytes)                                                   │
│  *signature = 0x4D42;                      // &#x27;BM&#x27; in              │
│  little-endian                                                               │
│  header[0] = file_size;                    // File size                      │
│  header[1] = 0;                            // Reserved                       │
│  header[2] = 54;                           // Offset to pixel data           │
│                                                                              │
│  // DIB header (40 bytes)                                                    │
│  header[3] = 40;                           // DIB header size                │
│  header[4] = width;                        // Width                          │
│  header[5] = -height;                      // Height (negative for           │
│  top-down)                                                                   │
│                                                                              │
│  // Color planes (16-bit) and bits per pixel (16-bit)                        │
│  uint16_t* color_data = (uint16_t*)(bmp + 26);                               │
│  color_data[0] = 1;                        // Color planes                   │
│  color_data[1] = 32;                       // Bits per pixel (32-bit BGRA)   │
│                                                                              │
│  header[7] = 0;                            // No compression                 │
│  header[8] = image_size;                   // Image size                     │
│  header[9] = 0;                            // X pixels per meter             │
│  header[10] = 0;                           // Y pixels per meter             │
│  header[11] = 0;                           // Total colors                   │
│  header[12] = 0;                           // Important colors               │
│                                                                              │
│  // Write pixel data directly (convert RGBA to BGRA)                         │
│  uint32_t* pixel_data = (uint32_t*)(bmp + 54);                               │
│  for (int y = 0; y &lt; height; y++) {                                       │
│      for (int x = 0; x &lt; width; x++) {                                    │
│          uint32_t pixel = buffer[y * width + x];                             │
│          // Swap R and B (RGBA to BGRA)                                      │
│          *pixel_data++ = pixel;                                              │
│      }                                                                       │
│  }                                                                           │
│                                                                              │
│  // Write to file                                                            │
│  FILE* file = fopen(filename, &quot;wb&quot;);                               │
│  if (!file) {                                                                │
│      fprintf(stderr, &quot;Cannot open file %s\n&quot;, filename);           │
│      free(bmp);                                                              │
│      return;                                                                 │
│  }                                                                           │
│                                                                              │
│  size_t written = fwrite(bmp, 1, file_size, file);                           │
│  if (written != file_size) {                                                 │
│      fprintf(stderr, &quot;Error writing file: %zu of %u bytes               │
│  written\n&quot;, written, file_size);                                       │
│  }                                                                           │
│                                                                              │
│  fclose(file);                                                               │
│  free(bmp-2);                                                                │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│ }                                                                            │
│                                                                              │

│ void DG_SleepMs(uint32_t ms) { // Spinloop implementation for sleep using    │
│ inline assembly // Assuming 3GHz processor (3 billion cycles per second) //  │
│ 1ms = 3 million cycles uint64_t cycles_per_ms = 3; uint64_t total_cycles =   │
│ cycles_per_ms * ms;                                                          │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│  volatile uint64_t i;                                                        │
│  for (i = 0; i &lt; total_cycles; i++)                                       │
│  {                                                                           │
│      // Use volatile inline assembly to ensure the loop isn&#x27;t           │
│  optimized away                                                              │
│      __asm__ volatile(&quot;&quot; ::: &quot;memory&quot;);                  │
│  }                                                                           │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│ }                                                                            │
│                                                                              │
│ int ms_ctr = 0;; uint32_t DG_GetTicksMs() { return ms_ctr++; }               │
│                                                                              │
│ int DG_GetKey(int* pressed, unsigned char* doomKey) { if                     │
│ (s_KeyQueueReadIndex == s_KeyQueueWriteIndex) { //key queue is empty return  │
│ 0; } else { unsigned short keyData = s_KeyQueue[s_KeyQueueReadIndex];        │
│ s_KeyQueueReadIndex++; s_KeyQueueReadIndex %= KEYQUEUE_SIZE;                 │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │

│      *doomKey = keyData &amp; 0xFF;                                          │
│                                                                              │
│      return 1;                                                               │
│  }                                                                           │

│                                                                              │
│ }                                                                            │

│ finish_reason='stop' content='Findings (concise, for the solver)             │
│ [solver]\n\n- Repository layout / important files\n  -                       │
│ /app/doomgeneric/doomgeneric/doomgeneric_img.c — provided image backend;     │
│ prints "DoomGeneric initialized..." and writes frames to /tmp/frame.bmp via  │
│ writeBMPFile("/tmp/frame.bmp", ...). It contains a main() that calls         │
│ doomgeneric_Create(argc, argv) and then an infinite doomgeneric_Tick()       │
│ loop.\n  - /app/doomgeneric/doomgeneric/doomgeneric.c — defines              │
│ doomgeneric_Create (allocates DG_ScreenBuffer, calls DG_Init and             │
│ D_DoomMain).\n  - Many other doom source files exist in                      │
│ /app/doomgeneric/doomgeneric/ (d_main.c, i_video.c, r_.c, etc.). The         │
│ Makefile lists all source/object files normally linked for the full          │
│ binary.\n  - /app/vm.js — the MIPS VM/ELF loader that will run the MIPS ELF. │
│ It expects a file named doomgeneric_mips in CWD (/app).\n\n- What vm.js      │
│ expects from the ELF (important)\n  - runElf("doomgeneric_mips") parses the  │
│ ELF file and:\n    - requires valid ELF header and section headers.\n    -   │
│ looks for .text, .data, .rodata sections and will load their bytes into an   │
│ in-memory word map (4-byte words, little-endian).\n    - requires at least   │
│ one symbol table section (SHT_SYMTAB). It parses symbol tables and collects  │
│ symbols; it treats STT_FUNC symbols as functions and records their address & │
│ size (used for function bounds mapping).\n    - selects the program entry    │
│ point: if a symbol named "main" exists, it uses that symbol&#x27;s address   │
│ as the entry point (entry_point returned to VM).\n  - After runElf returns,  │
│ vm.memory is set to the parsed memory mapping and vm.runProgram(entry_point) │
│ is called to start the MIPS interpreter.\n\n- MIPS VM runtime environment    │
│ (syscalls & behavior)\n  - The MIPS VM implements registers, instruction     │
│ emulation, and a syscall handler (handleSyscall).\n  - Implemented syscalls  │
│ (as used by typical C programs):\n    - SYS_read (0) — implemented for stdin │
│ and cached file reads.\n    - SYS_write (1) — implemented: writes to mapped  │
│ fd (stdout/stderr) via Node.js, or caches writes for regular files           │
│ (fileCache) to be flushed on close.\n    - SYS_open (2), SYS_close (3) —     │
│ implemented (open translates basic flags to Node fs flags; file caching      │
│ logic exists).\n    - SYS_lseek (8) — supported.\n    - SYS_time (201) —     │
│ supported.\n    - Many other syscalls are stubbed/unimplemented and return   │
│ error.\n  - VM maps file descriptors 0/1/2 to host stdin/stdout/stderr; open │
│ returns host fd numbers and uses a fileCache for regular files (written to   │
│ on close).\n  - Therefore the MIPS program can print to stdout (via          │
│ write/printf) and open and write files (e.g., /tmp/frame.bmp) and the VM     │
│ will handle those.\n\n- Build environment on the container\n  - Node.js      │
│ present (v18.20.4) — vm.js is runnable.\n  - Native compilers present:       │
│ /usr/bin/gcc and clang (x86_64). No MIPS cross-compilers found (searched for │
│ mips cross bins — none).\n  - readelf exists.\n  - doom.wad is present in    │
│ /app (large file).\n  - No prebuilt doomgeneric_mips present.\n\n-           │
│ Implications / options to produce doomgeneric_mips\n  - The VM requires a    │
│ real MIPS ELF with:\n    - correct ELF header (class=32), little-endian      │
│ preferred (vm loads words via readUInt32LE).\n    - .text/.data/.rodata      │
│ sections.\n    - a symbol table (SHT_SYMTAB) containing a &#x27;main&#x27;   │
│ symbol (STT_FUNC) so runElf picks the entry point.\n    - machine code in    │
│ .text that the MIPS interpreter can execute (MIPS32 instructions).\n  - Two  │

│ Findings (concise, for the solver) [solver]                                  │
│                                                                              │
│  • Repository layout / important files                                       │
│     • /app/doomgeneric/doomgeneric/doomgeneric_img.c — provided image        │
│       backend; prints "DoomGeneric initialized..." and writes frames to      │
│       /tmp/frame.bmp via writeBMPFile("/tmp/frame.bmp", ...). It contains a  │
│       main() that calls doomgeneric_Create(argc, argv) and then an infinite  │
│       doomgeneric_Tick() loop.                                               │
│     • /app/doomgeneric/doomgeneric/doomgeneric.c — defines                   │
│       doomgeneric_Create (allocates DG_ScreenBuffer, calls DG_Init and       │
│       D_DoomMain).                                                           │
│     • Many other doom source files exist in /app/doomgeneric/doomgeneric/    │
│       (d_main.c, i_video.c, r_*.c, etc.). The Makefile lists all             │
│       source/object files normally linked for the full binary.               │
│     • /app/vm.js — the MIPS VM/ELF loader that will run the MIPS ELF. It     │
│       expects a file named doomgeneric_mips in CWD (/app).                   │
│  • What vm.js expects from the ELF (important)                               │
│     • runElf("doomgeneric_mips") parses the ELF file and:                    │
│        • requires valid ELF header and section headers.                      │
│        • looks for .text, .data, .rodata sections and will load their bytes  │
│          into an in-memory word map (4-byte words, little-endian).           │
│        • requires at least one symbol table section (SHT_SYMTAB). It parses  │
│          symbol tables and collects symbols; it treats STT_FUNC symbols as   │
│          functions and records their address & size (used for function       │
│          bounds mapping).                                                    │
│        • selects the program entry point: if a symbol named "main" exists,   │
│          it uses that symbol's address as the entry point (entry_point       │
│          returned to VM).                                                    │
│     • After runElf returns, vm.memory is set to the parsed memory mapping    │
│       and vm.runProgram(entry_point) is called to start the MIPS             │
│       interpreter.                                                           │
│  • MIPS VM runtime environment (syscalls & behavior)                         │
│     • The MIPS VM implements registers, instruction emulation, and a syscall │
│       handler (handleSyscall).                                               │
│     • Implemented syscalls (as used by typical C programs):                  │
│        • SYS_read (0) — implemented for stdin and cached file reads.         │
│        • SYS_write (1) — implemented: writes to mapped fd (stdout/stderr)    │
│          via Node.js, or caches writes for regular files (fileCache) to be   │
│          flushed on close.                                                   │
│        • SYS_open (2), SYS_close (3) — implemented (open translates basic    │
│          flags to Node fs flags; file caching logic exists).                 │
│        • SYS_lseek (8) — supported.                                          │
│        • SYS_time (201) — supported.                                         │
│        • Many other syscalls are stubbed/unimplemented and return error.     │
│     • VM maps file descriptors 0/1/2 to host stdin/stdout/stderr; open       │
│       returns host fd numbers and uses a fileCache for regular files         │
│       (written to on close).                                                 │
│     • Therefore the MIPS program can print to stdout (via write/printf) and  │
│       open and write files (e.g., /tmp/frame.bmp) and the VM will handle     │
│       those.                                                                 │
│  • Build environment on the container                                        │
│     • Node.js present (v18.20.4) — vm.js is runnable.                        │
│     • Native compilers present: /usr/bin/gcc and clang (x86_64). No MIPS     │
│       cross-compilers found (searched for mips* cross bins — none).          │
│     • readelf exists.                                                        │
│     • doom.wad is present in /app (large file).                              │
│     • No prebuilt doomgeneric_mips present.                                  │
│  • Implications / options to produce doomgeneric_mips                        │
│     • The VM requires a real MIPS ELF with:                                  │
│        • correct ELF header (class=32), little-endian preferred (vm loads    │
│          words via readUInt32LE).                                            │
│        • .text/.data/.rodata sections.                                       │
│        • a symbol table (SHT_SYMTAB) containing a 'main' symbol (STT_FUNC)   │
│          so runElf picks the entry point.                                    │
│        • machine code in .text that the MIPS interpreter can execute (MIPS32 │
│          instructions).                                                      │
│     • Two main approaches:                                                   │
│        1 Cross-compile the real doomgeneric program (or a small wrapper) to  │
│          MIPS ELF:                                                           │
│           • Install a MIPS cross-toolchain (example: mipsel-linux-gnu-gcc or │
│             mips-linux-gnu-gcc) and build with appropriate CFLAGS (e.g.,     │
│             -march=mips32 -mabi=32 -EL for little-endian), ensure symbol     │
│             table is kept (do not strip).                                    │
│           • Use the existing Makefile but set CC and/or CROSS_COMPILE to the │
│             MIPS toolchain, and link producing doomgeneric_mips in /app.     │
│           • This approach may require building many files (full codebase),   │
│             but guarantees functionality.                                    │
│        2 Produce a small hand-compiled MIPS ELF with required symbols:       │

│             symbol table and real MIPS instructions.                         │

│       installing one is necessary to compile real MIPS executables.          │
│  • Specific bits the solver will need to handle                              │

│       words with readUInt32LE and assumes little-endian when loading         │
│       sections).                                                             │
│     • Ensure a symbol table with STT_FUNC entries (particularly 'main') is   │

│ // BMP file header structure typedef struct { uint16_t type;              // │
│ Magic identifier: 0x4d42 uint32_t size;              // File size in bytes   │
│ uint16_t reserved1;         // Not used uint16_t reserved2;         // Not   │
│ used uint32_t offset;            // Offset to image data in bytes uint32_t   │
│ dib_header_size;   // DIB Header size in bytes int32_t  width_px;            │
│ // Width of the image int32_t  height_px;         // Height of image         │
│ uint16_t num_planes;        // Number of color planes uint16_t               │
│ bits_per_pixel;    // Bits per pixel uint32_t compression;       //          │
│ Compression type uint32_t image_size_bytes;  // Image size in bytes int32_t  │
│ x_resolution_ppm;  // Pixels per meter int32_t  y_resolution_ppm;  // Pixels │
│ per meter uint32_t num_colors;        // Number of colors uint32_t           │
│ important_colors;  // Important colors } BMPHeader;                          │
│                                                                              │
│ #define KEYQUEUE_SIZE 16                                                     │
│                                                                              │
│ static unsigned short s_KeyQueue[KEYQUEUE_SIZE]; static unsigned int         │
│ s_KeyQueueWriteIndex = 0; static unsigned int s_KeyQueueReadIndex = 0;       │
│                                                                              │
│ static unsigned char convertToDoomKey(unsigned int key) { switch (key) {     │
│ case '\r':  // Return key key = KEY_ENTER; break; case 27:    // Escape key  │
│ key = KEY_ESCAPE; break; case 0x4B:  // Left arrow key = KEY_LEFTARROW;      │
│ break; case 0x4D:  // Right arrow key = KEY_RIGHTARROW; break; case 0x48:    │
│ // Up arrow key = KEY_UPARROW; break; case 0x50:  // Down arrow key =        │
│ KEY_DOWNARROW; break; case 0x1D:  // Ctrl key = KEY_FIRE; break; case ' ':   │
│ // Space key = KEY_USE; break; case 0x2A:  // Left Shift case 0x36:  //      │
│ Right Shift key = KEY_RSHIFT; break; default: key = (key); break; }          │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│  return key;                                                                 │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│ }                                                                            │
│                                                                              │
│ static void addKeyToQueue(int pressed, unsigned int keyCode) { unsigned char │
│ key = convertToDoomKey(keyCode);                                             │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│  unsigned short keyData = (pressed &lt;&lt; 8) | key;                        │
│                                                                              │
│  s_KeyQueue[s_KeyQueueWriteIndex] = keyData;                                 │
│  s_KeyQueueWriteIndex++;                                                     │
│  s_KeyQueueWriteIndex %= KEYQUEUE_SIZE;                                      │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│ }                                                                            │
│                                                                              │
│ // Function to write BMP file static void writeBMPFile(const char* filename, │
│ uint32_t* buffer, int width, int height) { uint32_t image_size = width *     │
│ height * 4;  // 4 bytes per pixel (BGRA) uint32_t file_size = 54 +           │
│ image_size;      // Headers + image data                                     │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│  // Allocate and initialize the buffer to 0                                  │
│  uint8_t* bmp = (uint8_t*)malloc(file_size+2);                               │
│  for (int i = 0; i &lt; 1024; i++) {                                         │
│    bmp[i] = 0;                                                               │
│  }                                                                           │
│  if (!bmp) {                                                                 │
│      fprintf(stderr, &quot;Memory allocation failed\n&quot;);                │
│      return;                                                                 │
│  }                                                                           │
│                                                                              │
│  bmp = bmp+2;                                                                │
│                                                                              │
│  // Use uint32_t array for headers (with proper byte offsets)                │
│  uint16_t* signature = (uint16_t*)bmp;                                       │
│  uint32_t* header = (uint32_t*)(bmp + 2);                                    │
│                                                                              │
│  // File header (14 bytes)                                                   │
│  *signature = 0x4D42;                      // &#x27;BM&#x27; in              │
│  little-endian                                                               │
│  header[0] = file_size;                    // File size                      │
│  header[1] = 0;                            // Reserved                       │
│  header[2] = 54;                           // Offset to pixel data           │
│                                                                              │
│  // DIB header (40 bytes)                                                    │
│  header[3] = 40;                           // DIB header size                │
│  header[4] = width;                        // Width                          │
│  header[5] = -height;                      // Height (negative for           │
│  top-down)                                                                   │
│                                                                              │
│  // Color planes (16-bit) and bits per pixel (16-bit)                        │
│  uint16_t* color_data = (uint16_t*)(bmp + 26);                               │
│  color_data[0] = 1;                        // Color planes                   │
│  color_data[1] = 32;                       // Bits per pixel (32-bit BGRA)   │
│                                                                              │
│  header[7] = 0;                            // No compression                 │
│  header[8] = image_size;                   // Image size                     │
│  header[9] = 0;                            // X pixels per meter             │
│  header[10] = 0;                           // Y pixels per meter             │
│  header[11] = 0;                           // Total colors                   │
│  header[12] = 0;                           // Important colors               │
│                                                                              │
│  // Write pixel data directly (convert RGBA to BGRA)                         │
│  uint32_t* pixel_data = (uint32_t*)(bmp + 54);                               │
│  for (int y = 0; y &lt; height; y++) {                                       │
│      for (int x = 0; x &lt; width; x++) {                                    │
│          uint32_t pixel = buffer[y * width + x];                             │
│          // Swap R and B (RGBA to BGRA)                                      │
│          *pixel_data++ = pixel;                                              │
│      }                                                                       │
│  }                                                                           │
│                                                                              │
│  // Write to file                                                            │
│  FILE* file = fopen(filename, &quot;wb&quot;);                               │
│  if (!file) {                                                                │
│      fprintf(stderr, &quot;Cannot open file %s\n&quot;, filename);           │
│      free(bmp);                                                              │
│      return;                                                                 │
│  }                                                                           │
│                                                                              │
│  size_t written = fwrite(bmp, 1, file_size, file);                           │
│  if (written != file_size) {                                                 │
│      fprintf(stderr, &quot;Error writing file: %zu of %u bytes               │
│  written\n&quot;, written, file_size);                                       │
│  }                                                                           │
│                                                                              │
│  fclose(file);                                                               │
│  free(bmp-2);                                                                │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│ }                                                                            │
│                                                                              │

│ short)); // No X11 initialization needed printf("DoomGeneric initialized.    │
│ Frames will be saved to /tmp/frame.bmp\n"); }                                │

│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│ }                                                                            │

│ void DG_SleepMs(uint32_t ms) { // Spinloop implementation for sleep using    │
│ inline assembly // Assuming 3GHz processor (3 billion cycles per second) //  │
│ 1ms = 3 million cycles uint64_t cycles_per_ms = 3; uint64_t total_cycles =   │
│ cycles_per_ms * ms;                                                          │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│  volatile uint64_t i;                                                        │
│  for (i = 0; i &lt; total_cycles; i++)                                       │
│  {                                                                           │
│      // Use volatile inline assembly to ensure the loop isn&#x27;t           │
│  optimized away                                                              │
│      __asm__ volatile(&quot;&quot; ::: &quot;memory&quot;);                  │
│  }                                                                           │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│ }                                                                            │

│ 0xFFFF; // Ensure it's 16 bits return (value & 0x8000) ? (value |            │
│ 0xFFFF0000) : value; }                                                       │
