│ (instruction >> 26) & 0x3F;                                                  │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│  // Process based on opcode                                                  │
│  switch (opcode) {                                                           │
│    case 0x00: // R-type instructions                                         │
│      return this.runRTypeInstruction(instruction);                           │
│                                                                              │
│    case 0x08: // ADDI                                                        │
│      {                                                                       │
│        const rs = (instruction &gt;&gt; 21) &amp; 0x1F;                      │
│        const rt = (instruction &gt;&gt; 16) &amp; 0x1F;                      │
│        const imm = instruction &amp; 0xFFFF;                                 │
│        this.addi(rt, rs, imm);                                               │
│      }                                                                       │
│      break;                                                                  │
│                                                                              │
│    case 0x09: // ADDIU                                                       │
│      {                                                                       │
│        const rs = (instruction &gt;&gt; 21) &amp; 0x1F;                      │
│        const rt = (instruction &gt;&gt; 16) &amp; 0x1F;                      │
│        const imm = instruction &amp; 0xFFFF;                                 │
│        this.addiu(rt, rs, imm);                                              │
│      }                                                                       │
│      break;                                                                  │
│                                                                              │
│    case 0x0c: // ANDI                                                        │
│      {                                                                       │
│        const rs = (instruction &gt;&gt; 21) &amp; 0x1F;                      │
│        const rt = (instruction &gt;&gt; 16) &amp; 0x1F;                      │
│        const imm = instruction &amp; 0xFFFF;                                 │
│        this.andi(rt, rs, imm);                                               │
│      }                                                                       │
│      break;                                                                  │
│                                                                              │
│    case 0x0f: // LUI                                                         │
│      {                                                                       │
│        const rt = (instruction &gt;&gt; 16) &amp; 0x1F;                      │
│        const imm = instruction &amp; 0xFFFF;                                 │
│        this.lui(rt, imm);                                                    │
│      }                                                                       │
│      break;                                                                  │
│                                                                              │
│    case 0x0d: // ORI                                                         │
│      {                                                                       │
│        const rs = (instruction &gt;&gt; 21) &amp; 0x1F;                      │
│        const rt = (instruction &gt;&gt; 16) &amp; 0x1F;                      │
│        const imm = instruction &amp; 0xFFFF;                                 │
│        this.ori(rt, rs, imm);                                                │
│      }                                                                       │
│      break;                                                                  │
│                                                                              │
│    case 0x0a: // SLTI                                                        │
│      {                                                                       │
│        const rs = (instruction &gt;&gt; 21) &amp; 0x1F;                      │
│        const rt = (instruction &gt;&gt; 16) &amp; 0x1F;                      │
│        const imm = instruction &amp; 0xFFFF;                                 │
│        this.slti(rt, rs, imm);                                               │
│      }                                                                       │
│      break;                                                                  │
│                                                                              │
│    case 0x0b: // SLTIU                                                       │
│      {                                                                       │
│        const rs = (instruction &gt;&gt; 21) &amp; 0x1F;                      │
│        const rt = (instruction &gt;&gt; 16) &amp; 0x1F;                      │
│        const imm = instruction &amp; 0xFFFF;                                 │
│        this.sltiu(rt, rs, imm);                                              │
│      }                                                                       │
│      break;                                                                  │
│                                                                              │
│    case 0x0e: // XORI                                                        │
│      {                                                                       │
│        const rs = (instruction &gt;&gt; 21) &amp; 0x1F;                      │
│        const rt = (instruction &gt;&gt; 16) &amp; 0x1F;                      │
│        const imm = instruction &amp; 0xFFFF;                                 │
│        this.xori(rt, rs, imm);                                               │
│      }                                                                       │
│      break;                                                                  │
│                                                                              │
│    case 0x04: // BEQ                                                         │
│      {                                                                       │
│        const rs = (instruction &gt;&gt; 21) &amp; 0x1F;                      │
│        const rt = (instruction &gt;&gt; 16) &amp; 0x1F;                      │
│        const offset = this.signExtend16(instruction &amp; 0xFFFF);           │
│        this.beq(rs, rt, offset);                                             │
│      }                                                                       │
│      break;                                                                  │
│                                                                              │
│    case 0x01: // BGEZ, BLTZ family                                           │
│      {                                                                       │
│        const rs = (instruction &gt;&gt; 21) &amp; 0x1F;                      │
│        const op = (instruction &gt;&gt; 16) &amp; 0x1F; // Actually the      │
│  operation code                                                              │
│        const offset = this.signExtend16(instruction &amp; 0xFFFF);           │
│                                                                              │
│        switch (op) {                                                         │
│          case 0x00: this.bltz(rs, offset); break;     // BLTZ                │
│          case 0x01: this.bgez(rs, offset); break;     // BGEZ                │
│          case 0x10: this.bltzal(rs, offset); break;   // BLTZAL              │
│          case 0x11: this.bgezal(rs, offset); break;   // BGEZAL              │
│          default:                                                            │
│            throw new Error(`Unknown branch operation:                        │
│  0x${op.toString(16)}`);                                                     │
│        }                                                                     │
│      }                                                                       │
│      break;                                                                  │
│                                                                              │
│    case 0x07: // BGTZ                                                        │
│      {                                                                       │
│        const rs = (instruction &gt;&gt; 21) &amp; 0x1F;                      │
│        const offset = this.signExtend16(instruction &amp; 0xFFFF);           │
│        this.bgtz(rs, offset);                                                │
│      }                                                                       │
│      break;                                                                  │
│                                                                              │
│    case 0x06: // BLEZ                                                        │
│      {                                                                       │
│        const rs = (instruction &gt;&gt; 21) &amp; 0x1F;                      │
│        const offset = this.signExtend16(instruction &amp; 0xFFFF);           │
│        this.blez(rs, offset);                                                │
│      }                                                                       │
│      break;                                                                  │
│                                                                              │
│    case 0x05: // BNE                                                         │
│      {                                                                       │
│        const rs = (instruction &gt;&gt; 21) &amp; 0x1F;                      │
│        const rt = (instruction &gt;&gt; 16) &amp; 0x1F;                      │
│        const offset = this.signExtend16(instruction &amp; 0xFFFF);           │
│        this.bne(rs, rt, offset);                                             │
│      }                                                                       │
│      break;                                                                  │
│                                                                              │
│    case 0x02: // J                                                           │
│      {                                                                       │
│        const target = instruction &amp; 0x3FFFFFF;                           │
│        this.j(target);                                                       │
│      }                                                                       │
│      break;                                                                  │
│                                                                              │
│    case 0x03: // JAL                                                         │
│      {                                                                       │
│        const target = instruction &amp; 0x3FFFFFF;                           │
│        this.jal(target);                                                     │
│      }                                                                       │
│      break;                                                                  │
│                                                                              │
│    case 0x20: // LB                                                          │
│      {                                                                       │
│        const rs = (instruction &gt;&gt; 21) &amp; 0x1F;                      │
│        const rt = (instruction &gt;&gt; 16) &amp; 0x1F;                      │
│        const offset = this.signExtend16(instruction &amp; 0xFFFF);           │
│        this.lb(rt, offset, rs);                                              │
│      }                                                                       │
│      break;                                                                  │
│                                                                              │
│    case 0x24: // LBU                                                         │
│      {                                                                       │
│        const rs = (instruction &gt;&gt; 21) &amp; 0x1F;                      │
│        const rt = (instruction &gt;&gt; 16) &amp; 0x1F;                      │
│        const offset = this.signExtend16(instruction &amp; 0xFFFF);           │
│        this.lbu(rt, offset, rs);                                             │
│      }                                                                       │
│      break;                                                                  │
│                                                                              │
│    case 0x21: // LH                                                          │
│      {                                                                       │
│        const rs = (instruction &gt;&gt; 21) &amp; 0x1F;                      │
│        const rt = (instruction &gt;&gt; 16) &amp; 0x1F;                      │
│        const offset = this.signExtend16(instruction &amp; 0xFFFF);           │
│        this.lh(rt, offset, rs);                                              │
│      }                                                                       │
│      break;                                                                  │
│                                                                              │
│    case 0x25: // LHU                                                         │
│      {                                                                       │
│        const rs = (instruction &gt;&gt; 21) &amp; 0x1F;                      │
│        const rt = (instruction &gt;&gt; 16) &amp; 0x1F;                      │
│        const offset = this.signExtend16(instruction &amp; 0xFFFF);           │
│        this.lhu(rt, offset, rs);                                             │
│      }                                                                       │
│      break;                                                                  │
│                                                                              │
│    case 0x23: // LW                                                          │
│      {                                                                       │
│        const rs = (instruction &gt;&gt; 21) &amp; 0x1F;                      │
│        const rt = (instruction &gt;&gt; 16) &amp; 0x1F;                      │
│        const offset = this.signExtend16(instruction &amp; 0xFFFF);           │
│        this.lw(rt, offset, rs);                                              │
│      }                                                                       │
│      break;                                                                  │
│                                                                              │
│    case 0x28: // SB                                                          │
│      {                                                                       │
│        const rs = (instruction &gt;&gt; 21) &amp; 0x1F;                      │
│        const rt = (instruction &gt;&gt; 16) &amp; 0x1F;                      │
│        const offset = this.signExtend16(instruction &amp; 0xFFFF);           │
│        this.sb(rt, offset, rs);                                              │
│      }                                                                       │
│      break;                                                                  │
│                                                                              │
│    case 0x29: // SH                                                          │
│      {                                                                       │
│        const rs = (instruction &gt;&gt; 21) &amp; 0x1F;                      │
│        const rt = (instruction &gt;&gt; 16) &amp; 0x1F;                      │
│        const offset = this.signExtend16(instruction &amp; 0xFFFF);           │
│        this.sh(rt, offset, rs);                                              │
│      }                                                                       │
│      break;                                                                  │
│                                                                              │
│    case 0x2b: // SW                                                          │
│      {                                                                       │
│        const rs = (instruction &gt;&gt; 21) &amp; 0x1F;                      │
│        const rt = (instruction &gt;&gt; 16) &amp; 0x1F;                      │
│        const offset = this.signExtend16(instruction &amp; 0xFFFF);           │
│        this.sw(rt, offset, rs);                                              │
│      }                                                                       │
│      break;                                                                  │
│                                                                              │
│    case 0x22: // LWL                                                         │
│      {                                                                       │
│        const rs = (instruction &gt;&gt; 21) &amp; 0x1F;                      │
│        const rt = (instruction &gt;&gt; 16) &amp; 0x1F;                      │
│        const offset = this.signExtend16(instruction &amp; 0xFFFF);           │
│        this.lwl(rt, offset, rs);                                             │
│      }                                                                       │
│      break;                                                                  │
│                                                                              │
│    case 0x26: // LWR                                                         │
│      {                                                                       │
│        const rs = (instruction &gt;&gt; 21) &amp; 0x1F;                      │
│        const rt = (instruction &gt;&gt; 16) &amp; 0x1F;                      │
│        const offset = this.signExtend16(instruction &amp; 0xFFFF);           │
│        this.lwr(rt, offset, rs);                                             │
│      }                                                                       │
│      break;                                                                  │
│                                                                              │
│    case 0x2a: // SWL                                                         │
│      {                                                                       │
│        const rs = (instruction &gt;&gt; 21) &amp; 0x1F;                      │
│        const rt = (instruction &gt;&gt; 16) &amp; 0x1F;                      │
│        const offset = this.signExtend16(instruction &amp; 0xFFFF);           │
│        this.swl(rt, offset, rs);                                             │
│      }                                                                       │
│      break;                                                                  │
│                                                                              │
│    case 0x2e: // SWR                                                         │
│      {                                                                       │
│        const rs = (instruction &gt;&gt; 21) &amp; 0x1F;                      │
│        const rt = (instruction &gt;&gt; 16) &amp; 0x1F;                      │
│        const offset = this.signExtend16(instruction &amp; 0xFFFF);           │
│        this.swr(rt, offset, rs);                                             │
│      }                                                                       │
│      break;                                                                  │
│                                                                              │
│  case 0x1F: // SPECIAL3                                                      │
│  {                                                                           │
│      const rs = (instruction &gt;&gt; 21) &amp; 0x1F;                        │
│      const rt = (instruction &gt;&gt; 16) &amp; 0x1F;                        │
│      const rd = (instruction &gt;&gt; 11) &amp; 0x1F;                        │
│      const function_field = instruction &amp; 0x3F; // Function field is     │
│  the lowest 6 bits                                                           │
│                                                                              │
│      // For BSHFL subfamily instructions (SEB, SEH)                          │
│      if (function_field === 0x20) { // BSHFL                                 │
│      const op = (instruction &gt;&gt; 6) &amp; 0x1F; // Extract operation    │
│  field                                                                       │
│                                                                              │
│      switch (op) {                                                           │
│      case 0x10: // SEB - Sign-Extend Byte                                    │
│          this.seb(rd, rt);                                                   │
│          break;                                                              │
│      case 0x18: // SEH - Sign-Extend Halfword                                │
│          this.seh(rd, rt);                                                   │
│          break;                                                              │
│      case 0x02: // WSBH                                                      │
│          this.wsbh(rd, rt);                                                  │
│          break;                                                              │
│      default:                                                                │
│          throw new Error(`Unknown BSHFL operation: 0x${op.toString(16)}`);   │
│      }                                                                       │
│      } else if (function_field === 0x00) { // EXT                            │
│      const pos = (instruction &gt;&gt; 6) &amp; 0x1F;                        │
│      const size_minus1 = (instruction &gt;&gt; 11) &amp; 0x1F;               │
│      const size = size_minus1 + 1;                                           │
│      this.ext(rt, rs, pos, size);                                            │
│      } else {                                                                │
│      throw new Error(`Unknown SPECIAL3 function:                             │
│  0x${function_field.toString(16)}`);                                         │
│      }                                                                       │
│      break;                                                                  │
│  }                                                                           │
│                                                                              │
│    case 0x1C: // SPECIAL2                                                    │
│      {                                                                       │

│                                                                              │
│  • Rather than attempt an immediate full cross-build of the whole Doom       │
│    source (large and likely to emit MIPS instructions the VM doesn't         │

│    vm.js to support any additional instructions) or expand the VM to support │
│    more MIPS instructions/glibc patterns.                                    │

│    instructions the VM didn't support (unknown SPECIAL3), so it was not      │
